- The IDF said that it killed two militants from Hezbollah's [Unit 1800](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Unit_1800), responsible for liaising with Palestinian militant groups, in Beirut.

  [^cite_note-344]

- Two UNIFIL peacekeepers were killed and a third was seriously injured when an explosion struck their vehicle near [Bani Haiyyan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bani_Haiyyan).

  [^cite_note-345]

- Four soldiers from the [Nahal Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nahal_Brigade)'s reconnaissance unit were killed and two others were wounded, one seriously, during a clash with Hezbollah militants in southern Lebanon, increasing the IDF death toll in Lebanon to 11.

  [^cite_note-346]

- An Israeli soccer team had to halt a training session due to the danger posed by a rocket that was fired from Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-347]

### 31 March

- Two people including a two-year-old boy were killed in an Israeli strike in Khan Yunis. Three people were killed in another Israeli strike in Jabalia. Another Israeli strike hit a group of people in the vicinity of a [police](81695807.md) checkpoint in al-Mawasi, killing at least one person and injuring eight others.

  [^cite_note-348]

- Israeli airstrikes in Jnah area of Beirut killed seven people and injured 26 others.

  [^cite_note-349]

  &#x20;The IDF said that an [Israeli Navy](1370788.md) strike in the capital killed Hajj Yusuf Ismail Hashem who served as the commander of Hezbollah's Southern Front and accused him of targeting Israeli civilians. The Lebanese group later confirmed his death, saying that he was killed alongside a group of second-tier and third-tier commanders.

  [^cite_note-350]

  [^cite_note-uni0104-351]

- The IDF said that its unit 300 has uncovered dozens of launchers loaded with rockets in Lebanon aimed at Israel.

  [^cite_note-352]

- Israeli media reported that Hezbollah launched 50 rockets on Israel,

  [^cite_note-353]

  &#x20;including 29 high-trajectory weapons and 17 attacks using UAVs.

  [^cite_note-354]

## April 2026

### 1 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said that at least 50 people were killed in Israeli strikes in Lebanon in the last 24 hours, increasing its count of the death toll in Lebanon since 2 March to 1,318.

  [^cite_note-355]

- The IDF said that it struck two currency exchange stores used by Hezbollah militants in Beirut.

  [^cite_note-uni0104-351]

- The Alma center reported that Hezbollah launched 33 waves of attacks on April 1st.

  [^cite_note-alma0304-356]

### 2 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said that at least 27 people were killed in Israeli strikes in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing its count of the Lebanese death toll since 2 March to 1,345.

  [^cite_note-357]

- [i24NEWS](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/I24NEWS_\(Israeli_TV_channel\)) reported that Hezbollah fired more than 130 rockets at Israel in the past day, and additional 260 of its rockets fell within Lebanon in its attempts to hit IDF soldiers.

  [^cite_note-358]

- The IDF said that it killed more than 40 Hezbollah militants in the past day.

  [^cite_note-359]

- The Alma center reported that Hezbollah launched 35 waves of attacks on April 2nd.

  [^cite_note-alma0304-356]

### 3 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said that at least 23 people were killed in Israeli strikes in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing its count of the Lebanese death toll since the start of the war to 1,368.

  [^cite_note-360]

- The IDF said that it has killed 1000 Hezbollah militants in the past month.

  [^cite_note-361]

- Rockets were reportedly fired from Lebanon at northern Israel in coordination with Iranian rockets.

  [^cite_note-362]

### 4 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said that at least 54 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing its count of the Lebanese death toll since the start of the war to 1,422.

  [^cite_note-363]

- An IDF soldier from the [210th Division](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/210th_Division_\(Israel\)) was killed and another was critically injured in a friendly fire incident during an overnight raid in southern Lebanon to capture a suspect believed to be aiding Hezbollah.

  [^cite_note-364]

- The IDF issued an evacuation order for the [Masnaa Border Crossing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Masnaa_Border_Crossing), saying that Hezbollah was using it for military purposes.

  [^cite_note-reuters0504-365]

### 5 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said that at least 39 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing its count of the Lebanese death toll since the start of the war to 1,461.

  [^cite_note-reuters0504-365]

- Local health authorities said that an Israeli strike hit a group of people in Jaffa Street in the vicinity of Gaza City's Daraj neighbourhood, killing four people and injuring others. The IDF said that it killed ⁠militants posing an immediate threat.

  [^cite_note-366]

- The Lebanese Army announced that an Israeli strike in south Lebanon killed one of its soldiers.

  [^cite_note-367]

- The IDF said that it found rocket launchers, [RPGs](26286.md), and [surface-to-surface missiles](1483499.md) allegedly stored in a school in Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-368]

- Since the end of February the IDF said that it has detected 165 rockets that were launched by Hezbollah and struck within UNIFIL bases or close to them.

  [^cite_note-369]

- Lebanon's health ministry said that seven people including a four-year-old girl were killed in an Israeli strike in [Kfar Hatta](56111570.md) following an Israeli overnight evacuation order instructing people to leave the village.

  [^cite_note-reuters0504-365]

- Shafaq News reported that an Israeli airstrike in Ain Saadeh, Beirut, killed three people, including head of the [Lebanese Forces](2113218.md)' center in [Yahchouch](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yahchouch) and his wife.

  [^cite_note-370]

  &#x20;The Lebanese Forces said that the strike targeted a member of the Quds Force, who apparently survived.

  [^cite_note-371]

### 6 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said that at least 36 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing its count of the Lebanese death toll since the start of the war to 1,497.

  [^cite_note-372]

- The Alma center reported that three Israeli civilians and 11 IDF soldiers were killed in the fighting in the Lebanese theater since the start of the war. It also reported that since 2 March, Hezbollah launched 1,121 waves of attacks towards Israel's territory, of which 70% were rocket attacks and 29% involved UAVs.

  [^cite_note-373]

### 7 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said that at least 33 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing its count of the Lebanese death toll since the start of the war to 1,530.

  [^cite_note-374]

- The Alma center reported that Hezbollah carried out 43 waves of attacks from Lebanon to Israel,

  [^cite_note-alma0904-375]

  &#x20;including 10 rockets that were fired at [Nahariya](323332.md) and injured two civilians, and 40 more rockets that were fired from Lebanon into Israeli communities.

  [^cite_note-376]

- Israeli Prime Minister Benjamin Netanyahu asserted that the [US-Israel-Iran ceasefire](82888027.md) does not apply to Lebanon, contradicting mediator Pakistani Prime Minister [Shehbaz Sharif](6502561.md)'s announcement.

  [^cite_note-377]

- An Israeli soldier from the [Golani Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golani_Brigade) was killed and five others were injured during a clash with Hezbollah militants in southern Lebanon, raising the IDF death toll to 12.

  [^cite_note-378]

### 8 April

- Hezbollah said that it halted attacks on Israel and on Israeli soldiers in Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-379]

- The IDF said that it had targeted more than a hundred Hezbollah assets including headquarters, intelligence centers, missile infrastructure, sites related to the Radwan Force and aerial and naval units,

  [^cite_note-380]

  &#x20;killing at least 250 Hezbollah militants.

  [^cite_note-381]

  [^cite_note-382]

  &#x20;Al Jazeera reported that the [strikes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/April_8,_2026,_Lebanon_attacks) killed at least 254 people and injured at least 1,165 others.

  [^cite_note-383]

  &#x20;In Beirut alone, at least 92 people were killed and at least 742 others were injured.

  [^cite_note-384]

  &#x20;Hezbollah claimed responsibility for launching rockets towards north Israel as a response to "cease-fire violations".

  [^cite_note-385]

- Iranian media said that Iran paused [Hormuz](29005.md) traffic over Israeli attacks in Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-386]

### 9 April

- Health and education officials in Gaza said that apparent Israeli fire killed a girl in Beit Lahia.

  [^cite_note-rts0904-387]

- Health officials in Gaza said that at least two people were killed in an Israeli strike in Jabalia, while one people was killed in another strike in Khan Yunis.

  [^cite_note-rts0904-387]

  &#x20;The IDF said that it targeted a militant in north Gaza.

  [^cite_note-388]

- The IDF said that it killed Ali Yusuf Harshi, the secretary and nephew of Hezbollah leader [Naim Qassem](28105310.md), in an airstrike in Beirut.

  [^cite_note-389]

- The IDF said that an airstrike in [Sidon](28366.md) the day prior killed Maher Qassem Hamdan, the commander of the [Lebanese Resistance Brigades](52552320.md) in [Shebaa](42907053.md), along with eight other militants.

  [^cite_note-390]

- The IDF and [Shin Bet](311752.md) said that they killed an al-Quds militant whom it accused of participating in the 7 October attacks and violating the ceasefire along with three other al-Qassam Brigades militants in a strike earlier this week in north Gaza.

  [^cite_note-391]

- The IDF issued evacuation order for southern suburbs of Beirut, saying that it is only targeting Hezbollah militants and military targets.

  [^cite_note-392]

- Israel Katz said that over 200 Hezbollah militants were killed in airstrikes the day prior.

  [^cite_note-393]

- The Alma center reported that Hezbollah carried out at least 15 waves of attacks from Lebanon to Israel.

  [^cite_note-alma0904-375]

### 10 April

- Hezbollah fired a missile at [Ashdod](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ashdod) in the early morning. The group said that it targeted [Ashdod Naval Base](77297669.md).

  [^cite_note-394]

  [^cite_note-395]

- [Mako](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mako_\(website\)) reported that two IDF soldiers were injured by shrapnel from an explosive drone which fell in their vicinity in south Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-mako1004-396]

- Mako reported that Hezbollah fired more than 100 rockets at Israel since the beginning of the [2026 Iran war ceasefire](82888027.md). It also reported that those wounded in a rocket barrage from Lebanon which hit the Karmiel area include a 4-month-old Israeli infant and four Israeli children.

  [^cite_note-mako1004-396]

- A Hezbollah rocket barrage towards northern Israel hit and damaged a 1,500 year old church in Nahariya.

  [^cite_note-397]

### 11 April

- At least 67 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll since the start of the war to 2,020.

  [^cite_note-398]

- Officials from Gaza's health ministry said that at least six people were killed when an Israeli strike hit a [police](81695807.md) checkpoint in Bureij camp. They also said that at least one person was killed in another Israeli strike in Beit Lahia. Several others were also wounded in those strikes. The IDF said that the strike in Bureij had killed militants of the al-Qassam Brigades that approached the yellow line,

  [^cite_note-399]

  &#x20;including a militant who participated in the 7 October attacks on Israel and took part in the kidnapping of Israelis and militants who held Israeli hostages.

  [^cite_note-400]

- Hezbollah militants fired 10 rockets at [Karmiel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karmiel),

  [^cite_note-401]

  &#x20;and fired additional 30 rocket at other locations in northern Israel, triggering repeated emergency sirens throughout the day.

  [^cite_note-402]

### 12 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said that at least 35 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing its count of the Lebanese death toll since the start of the war to 2,055 including 165 children.

  [^cite_note-403]

- The IDF said that it killed 20 Hezbollah militants during an operation in a hospital in [Bint Jbeil](6124393.md) that was allegedly used to store weapons and carry out surveillance and accused the group of using medical facilities and ambulances in Lebanon for military activities.

  [^cite_note-404]

- A Hezbollah rocket attack in southern Lebanon moderately injured two Israeli soldiers.

  [^cite_note-405]

### 13 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said that at least 34 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to their count since the start of the war to 2,089.

  [^cite_note-406]

- An Israeli woman was lightly injured in a rocket attack by Hezbollah militants in [Nahariya](323332.md).

  [^cite_note-407]

- An IDF [Humvee](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Humvee) overturned in southern Lebanon, killing a reservist and injuring three others.

  [^cite_note-408]

### 14 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said that at least 35 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to their count since the start of the war to 2,124.

  [^cite_note-409]

- Ten Israeli soldiers from the [35th Paratroopers Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/35th_Paratroopers_Brigade_\(Israel\)) were injured, three seriously, during an overnight battle with Hezbollah militants in [Bint Jbeil](6124393.md).

  [^cite_note-410]

- Israel and Lebanon held direct talks in [Washington, D.C.](108956.md) for the first time since 1993, aimed at establishing negotiations between the two countries to end the war.

  [^cite_note-411]

- The IDF said that three Hezbollah militants surrendered in Bint Jbeil, while the commander of the [401st Brigade](2072251.md)'s 52nd Battalion was seriously injured in the area.

  [^cite_note-412]

- The IDF said that a missile launcher that was aimed at Israel was uncovered in Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-413]

- Hezbollah fired 47 rockets at northern Israel and additional 17 rockets within Lebanon at IDF soldiers.

  [^cite_note-414]

### 15 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said at least 43 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count since the start of the war to 2,167.

  [^cite_note-415]

- The IDF said that more than 1,700 Hezbollah militants were killed in Lebanon since the end of February.

  [^cite_note-416]

- Hezbollah fired more than 40 rockets at Israel, injuring one person in [Tamra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tamra).

  [^cite_note-417]

- A Hezbollah rocket strike in southern Lebanon injured five Israeli soldiers, one seriously.

  [^cite_note-418]

### 16 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said at least 29 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count since the start of the war to 2,196 including 172 children.

  [^cite_note-419]

- Hezbollah fired a missile at central Israel, which fell in an open area.

  [^cite_note-420]

- The commander of the IDF's Unit 603 said that his forces destroyed more than 1,000 militant sites in Lebanon, many of which were residential houses that Hezbollah allegedly converted to weapon storage facilities.

  [^cite_note-421]

- Hezbollah rocket attacks seriously injured two Israelis near [Karmiel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karmiel) and injured another Israeli in [Nahariya](323332.md).

  [^cite_note-422]

- President Trump announced that Israel and Lebanon agreed to a 10-day [truce](82970933.md).

  [^cite_note-423]

- The Israeli military destroyed the Qasmieh Bridge hours before the ceasefire announcement.

  [^cite_note-424]

### 17 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said that the Lebanese death toll since March 2 increased according to its count to 2,294.

  [^cite_note-425]

- The Lebanese army alleged Israeli shelling in several villages in south Lebanon in breach of the [ceasefire](82970933.md).

  [^cite_note-426]

- Iran announced that passage of commercial vessels through the [Hormuz Strait](29005.md) is completely open during the truce in Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-427]

### 18 April

- Iran said that it closed the Strait of Hormuz again in response to the US refusing to lift [its naval blockade](82937256.md).

  [^cite_note-428]

- The IDF said that it carried out several strikes on Hezbollah militants whom it accused of violating the ceasefire by approaching its troops in the "Yellow Line" in southern Lebanon over the past day.

  [^cite_note-429]

- French president [Emmanuel Macron](43671127.md) said that a French soldier was killed and three others were injured in an attack on UNIFIL peacekeepers apparently conducted by Hezbollah. The group's leadership denied involvement. Macron later announced that another French soldier died from the injuries sustained in the attack.

  [^cite_note-430]

  [^cite_note-431]

- The IDF said that one of its reservists was killed and three other soldiers were injured during combat in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-432]

### 19 April

- The IDF said that one of its soldiers was killed and nine others were injured, one critically during combat in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-433]

### 21 April

- The IDF said that Hezbollah violated the ceasefire by launching rockets at an Israeli position in [Rab Thalathin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rab_Thalathin) and a drone into northern Israel. It added that it hit the rocket launcher as a response to one of those incidents. Hezbollah said that it fired rockets and attack drones towards a site in northern Israel that it said was the source of artillery shelling towards a town in south Lebanon in retaliation for what it said were Israeli violations of the truce.

  [^cite_note-434]

### 22 April

- Gaza health officials said that five people including three children were killed in an Israeli strike in Beit Lahia.

  [^cite_note-435]

- The Lebanese health ministry said that two people were killed in an Israeli strike on a car in southern Lebanon. Lebanese rescue workers said that one Lebanese female journalist was killed and another journalist was critically injured in a second Israeli strike that hit a house in the same area. The IDF said that individuals had violated the truce, endangering its forces.

  [^cite_note-436]

- Hezbollah said that it attacked an Israeli position in Al-Bayyada with a drone in retaliation to alleged Israeli violations of the truce. The IDF said that it intercepted the drone.

  [^cite_note-437]

  &#x20;The group added that it launched a drone attack on Israeli troops and a Humvee in [al-Qantara](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Qantara,_Marjayoun) in retaliation for Israeli attacks and downed four Israeli reconnaissance drones in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-438]

### 23 April

- Shafaq reported that two people, including a child were injured due to Israeli artillery fire on [Yatar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yatar).

  [^cite_note-shafaq2304-439]

- The IDF said that Hezbollah militants launched a guided anti-tank missile near troops in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-440]

  &#x20;It later said that the group launched an explosive-laden drone attack on troops in southern Lebanon, lightly injuring one soldier, and launched several rockets at an IDF position in [Aynata](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aynata).

  [^cite_note-toi2304-441]

- Shafaq reported that an Israeli strike on a car in [Nabatieh](43794798.md) killed three people.

  [^cite_note-shafaq2304-439]

  &#x20;The IDF said that it killed three Hezbollah militants who attempted to shoot down an Israeli drone above southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-toi2304-441]

- Hezbollah militants fired four rockets at [Shtula](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shtula), causing no damage or injuries. The IDF said it retaliated by striking structures used by Hezbollah in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-442]

  [^cite_note-443]

- President Trump said that Israel and Lebanon agreed to a three week extension of the [ceasefire](82970933.md).

  [^cite_note-444]

### 24 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said that the Lebanese death toll since the start of the war according to its count increased to 2,491.

  [^cite_note-445]

- Two people were killed in an Israeli strike in Gaza City. The IDF said it had targeted two militants who threatened its forces in the area. Seven people including four [police](81695807.md) officers were killed in an Israeli strike on a police vehicle in Khan Yunis. Israeli strikes in northern Gaza killed a pregnant woman and her 4 and 13 year-old children. The IDF said that several militants had threatened its forces in the area and it targeted them with an airstrike after warning civilians.

  [^cite_note-446]

- The IDF issued evacuation orders for [Deir Aames](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deir_Aames), saying that Hezbollah militants are using the area for military activities.

  [^cite_note-447]

- The IDF said that it downed a Hezbollah drone prior to entering Israel.

  [^cite_note-448]

- Lebanese media reported that Hezbollah shot down an Israeli drone over [Tyre](30796.md) with an anti-aircraft missile. The IDF later confirmed that Hezbollah shot down one of its UAVs using an anti-aircraft missile.

  [^cite_note-449]

  [^cite_note-450]

- The IDF said Hezbollah militants fired explosive-laden drones towards its forces in [Al-Qantara](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Qantara,_Marjayoun) and accused the group of violating the truce. The IDF said that it struck surveillance equipment at a Hezbollah rocket-launching site in [Kounin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kounin), saying that the equipment “posed a direct threat to the forces operating in the area.”

  [^cite_note-451]

- The IDF released video claiming to show Hezbollah militants using ambulances for military activity.

  [^cite_note-452]

- The UNIFIL said that a second Indonesian peacekeeper died due to injuries from an apparent Israeli attack.

  [^cite_note-453]

- The IDF said that it killed six Hezbollah militants during [clashes](82919076.md) in [Bint Jbeil](6124393.md) and accused the group of violating the truce.

  [^cite_note-454]

- Hezbollah said that it hit an Israeli Humvee in Al-Qantara in retaliation for alleged Israeli violations of the ceasefire.

  [^cite_note-455]

- The IDF said that a canine from the [Oketz Unit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oketz_Unit) was killed after revealing the location of a Hezbollah cell in [Bint Jbeil](6124393.md), adding that all six militants were killed within an hour and a half of their identification and accused the group of violating the truce.

  [^cite_note-456]

- The IDF said that Hezbollah militants fired more explosive-laden drones that hit next to its forces in southern Lebanon and accused the group of violating the truce. It added that it attacked several Hezbollah rocket launchers in [Yatar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yatar) and [Kafra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kafra,_Bint_Jbeil), which are situated north of the Lebanese areas held by Israelis, saying that they “posed a threat to IDF troops and Israeli civilians".

  [^cite_note-457]

### 25 April

- A kite suspected of flying in from Gaza into southern Israel was recovered by the IDF.

  [^cite_note-458]

- The IDF said that it downed a 'suspicious aerial target' in an area in southern Lebanon area where its forces operate.

  [^cite_note-459]

- An Israeli strike in southern Lebanon killed four people. The IDF said that those killed include three Hezbollah militants. The IDF said that Hezbollah launched two rockets towards Israel. The IDF added it had targeted loaded Hezbollah rocket launchers in three places in southern Lebanon and [Radwan Force](69191358.md) facilities. It restated its evacuation orders not to approach the [Litani River](1920925.md) area.

  [^cite_note-460]

  [^cite_note-461]

- The IDF said that it hit "Hezbollah infrastructure" in its "buffer zone" in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-462]

- Prime Minister Benjamin Netanyahu's office said that he ordered the IDF to "vigorously strike" Hezbollah targets in Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-haaretz2504-463]

- Several drones were launched from Lebanon towards northern Israel.

  [^cite_note-haaretz2504-463]

### 26 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said at least 13 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count since the start of the war to 2,509.

  [^cite_note-464]

- Gaza medics said that an Israeli strike in the vicinity of Al-Mughraqa killed one person, and two others in the vicinity of Gaza City. Apparent Israeli gunfire killed a woman in Khan Yunis. The IDF said it was unaware of the incident in Khan Yunis, adding added that it killed several al-Qassam Brigades militants.

  [^cite_note-465]

- The IDF ordered restrictions on movement in areas of southern Lebanon,

  [^cite_note-466]

  &#x20;and issued evacuation orders for several south Lebanon villages beyond its control, citing alleged truce violations by Hezbollah militants.

  [^cite_note-467]

- The IDF announced that it dismantled an al-Qassam Brigades [tunnel](43413500.md) measuring roughly 800 m (2,600 ft) in southern Gaza, east of the Yellow Line, and said that it found weapons there. It added that the underground route contained living quarters, military equipment, and weapons, including tactical vests and a rocket belonging to al-Quds Brigades.

  [^cite_note-468]

- The IDF announced that it targeted Hezbollah military structures in overnight strikes throughout southern Lebanon; explosive drones fired at its troops landed in open area.

  [^cite_note-469]

- The IDF announced that the IAF downed three drones prior to entering northern Israel.

  [^cite_note-470]

- The IDF announced that one of its soldiers was killed while six others were injured, including an officer and three troops who were critically injured, a soldier who was moderately injured and a soldier who was slightly injured in a Hezbollah drone attack in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-471]

### 27 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said at least 11 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count since the start of the war to 2,521.

  [^cite_note-472]

- Hezbollah leader [Naim Qassem](28105310.md) said that the group will not revert to the pre-March status and will respond to Israeli attacks.

  [^cite_note-473]

- The IDF said that it lost contact with a drone fired from Lebanon at northern Israel.

  [^cite_note-474]

- The IDF said it dismantled over 50 Hezbollah infrastructure sites in recent days, including one [Hezbollah underground compound](78024047.md), and allegedly recovered an arms storage facility inside a children's room containing explosives, Kalashnikov rifles, grenades, [RPGs](26286.md), machine guns and ammunition and accused Hezbollah militants of exploiting Lebanese civilians for their activities. Separately, the IDF announced it downed a droned fired by Hezbollah militants fired an explosive drone towards its troops operating in southern Lebanon the previous day.

  [^cite_note-475]

- The IDF said it killed three Hezbollah militants and hit its military structures in the vicinity of the front line in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-476]

- The IDF said it started conducting strikes against Hezbollah infrastructure in eastern Lebanon, expanding the scope of its bombing campaign during a [truce](82970933.md).

  [^cite_note-477]

  &#x20;Security sources told Reuters that attacks hit in the vicinity of Nabi Chit.

  [^cite_note-reuters2704-478]

- The IDF announced that its troops operating in the Israeli buffer zone in Lebanon were targeted by an "explosive drone" fired by Hezbollah, without causing casualties.

  [^cite_note-479]

  &#x20;Hezbollah said that it had targeted an Israeli tank in southern Lebanon using a drone.

  [^cite_note-reuters2704-478]

- The IDF announced that it struck over 20 Hezbollah-linked arms manufacturing, storage and rocket firing sites sites throughout the Beqaa Valley and southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-480]

### 28 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said at least 13 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count since the start of the war to 2,534.

  [^cite_note-481]

- Gaza Strip health officials said that a 9-year-old boy was killed in an Israeli strike in eastern Khan Yunis. The IDF said it had targeted a person who posed a threat to its forces by approaching the “yellow line”. Gaza medics also said that four people were killed in an Israeli strike on a vehicle in Gaza City. The IDF said the strike targeted what it said was a militant.

  [^cite_note-482]

### 29 April

- The Lebanese health ministry said at least 42 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count since the start of the war to 2,576.

  [^cite_note-483]

- The Lebanese Army announced that a Lebanese ‌soldier and his ‌brother were killed in an Israeli attack ‌in Bint ‌Jbeil.

  [^cite_note-484]

### 30 April

- At least three people were killed in an Israeli strike in the vicinity of Salahudeen road in central Gaza. One person was killed in another Israeli strike in the vicinity of a hospital in Deir Al-Balah.

  [^cite_note-485]

- After a Hezbollah drone intrusion in Moshav Shomera, a car caught fire.

  [^cite_note-486]

- The IDF said that Hezbollah shot down one of its drone with its missile in southern Lebanon. It added that its troops killed five Hezbollah militants who were operating near its forces on the previous day.

  [^cite_note-487]

- The IDF announced that it killed an al-Qassam Brigades militant in Gaza whom it accused of planning to conduct an imminent attack the previous day.

  [^cite_note-488]

- The IDF issued evacuation orders for eight villages outside its "buffer zone" in southern Lebanon, citing "Hezbollah infrastructure". The IDF then said that it destroyed missile firing positions used by the group.

  [^cite_note-489]

- Two IDF soldiers were moderately injured, 10 slightly in a Hezbollah drone strike in Moshav Shomera.

  [^cite_note-490]

- Two incendiary kites were reported by Israeli communities in the Gaza-border including Kissufim and Nahal Oz. The IDF announced that no dangerous devices were found attached to them.

  [^cite_note-491]

- The IDF issued evacuation orders for residents of 15 villages in the Nabatieh area, citing alleged Hezbollah militant activities. The IDF later announced that it carried out strikes on Hezbollah infrastructure.

  [^cite_note-492]

- The IDF announced that one of its soldiers was killed and another soldier was moderately injured in a drone strike in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-493]

- The Trump administration's Board of Peace accused Hamas of theft of aid, saying that it reduced from 90% to less than 1% due to its efforts since the truce in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-494]

- The IDF announced that one of its officers and one of its noncommissioned officers were moderately injured after an explosive drone detonated in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-495]

## May 2026

### 4 May

- The Lebanese health ministry said at least 17 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count since the start of the war to 2,696.

  [^cite_note-496]

- The IDF said it downed a drone in the vicinity of the Israel-Lebanon border.

  [^cite_note-497]

- The IDF ordered to evacuate four residential areas in [Tyre district](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tyre_district), citing alleged truce violations by Hezbollah militants. It later said that it carried out strikes on Hezbollah infrastructure.

  [^cite_note-498]

  [^cite_note-499]

- The IDF said that it killed 10 armed Hezbollah militants in the last two days in several areas in the vicinity of its troops in southern Lebanon, adding that its multi-dimensional unit dismantled a loaded launch position and infrastructure used to store weapons, while the IAF also conducted strikes on Hezbollah infrastructure overnight after anti-tank fire was launched at its forces, without causing casualties.

  [^cite_note-500]

- The IDF said that two soldiers from the Golani reconnaissance brigade were moderately injured during a clash with Hezbollah militants in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-501]

- The IDF ordered to evacuate six south Lebanon villages, citing alleged truce violations by Hezbollah militants.

  [^cite_note-502]

### 6 May

- Two people, including a son of Hamas leader Khalil Al-Hayya, were killed in an Israeli strike in Gaza City.

  [^cite_note-internazionale0605-503]

  [^cite_note-wp0705-504]

- An Israeli strike hit a vehicle in Al-Mawasi, killing a [Gaza Police](81695807.md) colonel and injuring at least 17 others.

  [^cite_note-internazionale0605-503]

- At least three people were killed in another Israeli strike in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-internazionale0605-503]

- The IDF said that it killed over 220 Hezbollah militants since 16 April.

  [^cite_note-aj0605-505]

- An Israeli strike was reported in [Ghobeiry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ghobeiry). The IDF said that it killed Ahmed Ghaleb Balout, the commander of the Radwan Force.

  [^cite_note-aj0605-505]

  [^cite_note-wp0705-504]

  [^cite_note-506]

- Four people were killed in an Israeli strike in the [Beqaa Valley](2162385.md).

  [^cite_note-aj0605-505]

- The IDF said it carried out strikes on Hezbollah targets in southern Lebanon, after ordering residents of 12 towns to evacuate.

  [^cite_note-aj0605-505]

- Hezbollah said that it a carried out several operations against Israeli forces in southern Lebanon, as well as attacks on northern Israel.

  [^cite_note-aj0605-505]

- NNA reported that an Israeli strike hit a home in [Ain Baal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ain_Baal).

  [^cite_note-aj0605-505]

- Three Israeli strikes were reported in [Dibbin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dibbin).

  [^cite_note-aj0605-505]

- Lebanese media reported that Israeli fighter jets conducted three attacks in Nabatieh.

  [^cite_note-aj0605-505]

- At least 13 people were killed in Israeli strikes in southern and eastern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-aj0605-505]

### 7 May

- Three Hamas-affiliated security forces were killed in an Israeli strike on a guard post. [Gaza Interior Ministry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Interior_\(Palestine\)) said that a fourth security officer was seriously injured in the strike. The IDF said it carried out the strike on a Hamas command center.

  [^cite_note-wp0705-504]

- An Israeli soldier was caught desecrating a statue of the [Virgin Mary](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Virgin_Mary) in the Lebanese town of [Debel](9814180.md) by placing a cigarette in the statue's mouth. The IDF said it will take action when he is identified.

  [^cite_note-507]

  [^cite_note-508]

- Four Israeli soldiers were injured by an explosion in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-509]

- The IDF said that it killed over 85 Hezbollah militants and carried out strikes on 180 sites used by the group in the past week.

  [^cite_note-aj0605-505]

  [^cite_note-wp0705-504]

### 8 May

- The Lebanese health ministry said at least 32 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count since the start of the war to 2,759.

  [^cite_note-510]

### 9 May

- The Lebanese health ministry said at least 39 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours.

  [^cite_note-511]

### 10 May

- The Lebanese health ministry said at least 87 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 48 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll since the start of the war according to its count to 2,846.

  [^cite_note-512]

### 11 May

- The Lebanese health ministry said at least 23 people were killed in Israeli attacks in Lebanon in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll since the start of the war to 2,869.

  [^cite_note-513]

### 12 May

- NNA reported that a Syrian man was killed and his wife was injured after an Israeli strike hit a motorcycle in Tyre's Hamadieh area.

  [^cite_note-514]

### 13 May

- The Lebanese health ministry said that Lebanese death toll since the start of the war increased to 2,896 including over 200 children.

  [^cite_note-515]

### 15 May

- Israeli strikes hit an apartment and a vehicle in Rimal area of Gaza City, killing seven people including three women and a child and wounding at least 50 others. Israeli forces said that they targeted Hamas leader in the Gaza Strip [Izz al-Din al-Haddad](79997859.md),

  [^cite_note-516]

  [^cite_note-517]

  &#x20;whose death was confirmed by Hamas the next day.

  [^cite_note-518]

- The IDF reported that a soldier of the Golani Brigade's 12th Battalion was killed when Hezbollah militants launched a mortar at his unit during combat in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-519]

- The [truce](82970933.md) was extended by Israel and Lebanon for another 45 days.

  [^cite_note-520]

### 18 May

- The Lebanese health ministry said that Lebanese death toll since the start of the war increased to 3,020.

  [^cite_note-521]

### 19 May

- The Lebanese health ministry reported that 22 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll to 3,042.

  [^cite_note-522]

- The IDF announced that a major was killed during combat in southern Lebanon, increasing the IDF death toll in southern Lebanon to 21.

  [^cite_note-523]

### 20 May

- The Lebanese health ministry reported that 31 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll to 3,073.

  [^cite_note-524]

- Hezbollah reported that fighting between the group and an Israeli force consisting of tanks and infantry occurred in the vicinity of Haddatha.

  [^cite_note-525]

- The IDF announced that a brigade commander was critically injured and a lieutenant colonel was slightly to moderately injured during combat due to a Hezbollah drone attack in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-526]

### 21 May

- An IDF combat photographer was critically injured and six other soldiers were injured, three of them moderately and three others slightly in a Hezbollah drone attack during combat in southern Lebanon, increasing number of soldiers critically injured from its Operational Documentation Division since the start of the Gaza war to two.

  [^cite_note-527]

### 22 May

- The Lebanese health ministry reported that the death toll from Israeli attacks in Lebanon had risen to 3,111.

  [^cite_note-528]

### 23 May

- The IDF ordered to evacuate an area of Tyre. It later struck two structures in and around the city.

  [^cite_note-529]

- The IDF announced that a soldier was killed, while two others were injured, one critically and one slightly by a Hezbollah drone in northern Israel near the border, increasing the IDF death toll since the start of the war in Lebanon to 22.

  [^cite_note-530]

  &#x20;Hezbollah later published a video of a night time drone strike on the soldiers in [Iskandarounah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iskandarounah).

  [^cite_note-531]

- The Alma center reported that the Al-Risala Association showed rescue operations in a multi-barrel rocket launcher that was struck by Israel.

  [^cite_note-532]

### 24 May

- The Lebanese health ministry reported that the death toll from Israeli attacks in Lebanon had risen according to its count to 3,123.

  [^cite_note-533]

### 25 May

- A six-year-old girl and a woman were killed and another girl was injured in an Israeli strike that hit a tent in al-Mawasi. Israeli forces said that they targeted a militant.

  [^cite_note-534]

- The IDF reported that a sergeant of the [Israeli Combat Engineering Corps](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_Combat_Engineering_Corps) was killed and another soldier was critically injured by a Hezbollah [FPV drone](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fiber_optic_drone) in southern Lebanon, increasing the number of IDF personnel killed in Lebanon to 23.

  [^cite_note-535]

### 26 May

- An Israeli strike hit people who came out of their houses when a [Palestinian militia backed by Israel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-Hamas_insurgency_in_Gaza) attempted to storm an area east of Maghazi camp, killing at least five people and injuring several others. Two people were killed and several others were injured in an Israeli strike on a vehicle in Khan Yunis. The IDF said that it was a "targeted strike".

  [^cite_note-536]

- An Israeli strike hit a building in Rimal area of Gaza City, killing three people including a woman and injuring over 20 others. Prime Minister Netanyahu announced that it targeted [Mohammed Odeh](83210587.md), the Hamas leader in the Gaza Strip.

  [^cite_note-537]

  &#x20;The next day, Gaza media outlets connected to Hamas said that he was killed alongside his wife and children.

  [^cite_note-538]

  &#x20;Hamas later confirmed his death.

  [^cite_note-539]

### 27 May

- 10 people including an al-Qassam Brigades militant, five children and an elderly person were killed and over 20 others were wounded in separate Israeli strikes in Gaza. The IDF said that its strikes in northern Gaza targeted two al-Qassam Brigades militants.

  [^cite_note-540]

- The IDF said that a projectile fired from Lebanon landed in an open area in Israel after sirens sounded in several areas in the north, but no casualties were reported.

  [^cite_note-541]

- The IDF reported that a female soldier of the [Givati Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Givati_Brigade) was killed and two reservists were injured, one critically and another moderately in a Hezbollah drone attack on a military position on the Israeli border, increasing the IDF death toll to 24.

  [^cite_note-542]

### 28 May

- Prime Minister Netanyahu declared that he ordered Israeli forces to take control of more of ⁠Gaza's territory, first by seizing 70%.

  [^cite_note-543]

- The IDF said it struck Hezbollah militants that intended to blow up Lebanon's strategic national dam.

  [^cite_note-544]

### 29 May

- The Lebanese health ministry reported that 142 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the past 72 hours, increasing the death toll in Lebanon according to its count to 3,355.

  [^cite_note-545]

- Prime Minister Netanyahu announced that Israeli forces had crossed the Litani river and were operating in Beirut and the Bekaa Valley, in what he characterised as a “tactical victory”.

  [^cite_note-546]

- Israeli forces announced that they killed an al-Qassam Brigades' deputy commander, Imad Hassan Hussein Aslim, who was involved in the 7 October attacks.

  [^cite_note-547]

### 30 May

- The Lebanese health ministry reported that at least 16 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 3,371.

  [^cite_note-548]

- Hezbollah fired several rockets towards northern Israel including a barrage at Nahariya for the first time in three weeks, causing panic to people including children on a beach in the city.

  [^cite_note-549]

  &#x20;One rocket reached Karmiel, marking the furthest fire into Israel since the roughly month long ceasefire.

  [^cite_note-550]

  &#x20;Another barrage caused damage in Kiryat Shmona. The IDF said that it downed a "launch" which entered into northern Israel from Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-551]

### 31 May

- An Israeli strike hit a cafe on the emergency seaport in Gaza City, killing two people and injuring 12 others.

  [^cite_note-552]

- The IDF announced that a sergeant of the Givati Brigade was killed and four other soldiers were slightly injured after a Hezbollah drone attack on an IDF post in southern Lebanon, increasing the IDF death toll to 26.

  [^cite_note-553]

- The IDF seized [Beaufort Castle](6853436.md).

  [^cite_note-554]

## June 2026

### 1 June

- The IDF said that one of its sergeants was killed, one of its soldiers was critically injured and two of its soldiers were slightly injured in a Hezbollah drone attack in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-555]

- The IDF said that one of its medical officers was killed while seven soldiers were injured by a Hezbollah drone during combat in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-556]

- Israel agreed not to hit Beirut's southern suburbs, and Hezbollah agreed not to attack Israel as part of a US proposal, with [the cease-fire framework](82970933.md) to be expanded to include entire Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-557]

- &#x20;The IDF detected rocket launches towards the [Galilee](12639.md), the [Golan](7515865.md), and IDF positions in Lebanon after President Trump announced the truce. Several Israeli strikes were also reported in Lebanon during the same time.

  [^cite_note-558]

  [^cite_note-559]

### 2 June

- Gaza medics said that at least four people were killed in Israeli strikes in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-560]

- The Lebanese health ministry reported that at least 35 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 3,468.

  [^cite_note-561]

- A prosecutor's declaration revealed that a Hamas militant who held for a decade the body of IDF hostage soldier [Oron Shaul](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oron_Shaul), was arrested by Israel 18 months ago, and provided the details for the extraction of the body.

  [^cite_note-562]

### 3 June

- The Lebanese health ministry reported that at least 48 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 3,516.

  [^cite_note-563]

- Israel and Lebanon agreed to renew [a ceasefire](82970933.md), mediated by the US, and plan to establish "pilot zones".

  [^cite_note-564]

- An Israeli strike in the vicinity of Mughraqa area in central Gaza killed one person. The IDF said they struck an individual acting suspiciously in the vicinity of its forces operating in an area under its control. Another Israeli strike in the courtyard of a home in the nearby Maghazi refugee camp killed two people.

  [^cite_note-565]

### 4 June

- The Lebanese health ministry reported that at least 10 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 3,526.

  [^cite_note-566]

- At least 10 people including two children and two women were killed in at least four separate Israeli strikes in Gaza City. Israeli forces said that those killed in the strikes include four senior Hamas militants that were members of an apparatus that provided intelligence assessments to Hamas leaders.

  [^cite_note-567]

- The IDF said that a Hezbollah attack hit IDF Northern Command chief [Rafi Milo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rafi_Milo)'s car after he stepped out of the vehicle in southern Lebanon apparently sometime in the past several weeks, no casualties were reported.

  [^cite_note-568]

- A Serbian peacekeeper was killed while two others were injured in a strike on a UNIFIL base in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-569]

- Hezbollah rejected the truce deal announced the previous day and instead demanded a comprehensive truce and full withdrawal of Israel from Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-570]

- The IDF said one of its soldiers was killed when a Hezbollah militant launched an anti-tank missile on a tank north of the Litani River.

  [^cite_note-571]

### 6 June

- The Lebanese Health Ministry announced that the Lebanese death toll as a result of Israeli attacks had risen according to its count to 3,593.

  [^cite_note-572]

- An Israeli strike hit a large tent encampment in the heart of Gaza City, killing seven people including two women and injuring 15 others including children. An IDF spokesperson said the strike targeted militants.

  [^cite_note-573]

- Three Lebanese soldiers were killed in an IDF attack on their car near [Kfar Tebnit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kfar_Tebnit). The IDF said that the car was "moving suspiciously towards forces" and gunfire was reported in the area.

  [^cite_note-574]

- The IDF said that two of its soldiers were killed in combat in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-575]

### 7 June

- Gaza health officials said that Israeli strikes hit a [Gaza police](81695807.md) station and a vehicle in Gaza, killing at least nine people and injuring 20 others. The IDF said that one of those strikes targeted Hamas infrastructure.

  [^cite_note-576]

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 20 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 3,613.

  [^cite_note-577]

- The IDF announced that it downed two projectiles which entered from Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-578]

- Lebanese ⁠state media said that an Israeli strike in the southern suburbs of Beirut killed two people and wounded 11 others. The IDF said that it targeted Hezbollah infrastructure after the group fired towards northern Israel.

  [^cite_note-579]

### 8 June

- Six people including a child were killed in an Israeli strike that hit a tent in Mawasi.

  [^cite_note-580]

### 9 June

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that the death toll from Israeli attacks in Lebanon had risen according to its count to 3,666.

  [^cite_note-581]

- A militant of unclear affiliation was killed after crossing into northern Israel from Lebanon and firing at IDF soldiers, causing no injuries.

  [^cite_note-toi0906-582]

- Israeli airstrikes in [Tyre](30796.md) killed eight people and injured 32 others after the IDF issued an evacuation order due to alleged presence of Hezbollah militants in the entire city, including its Christian quarter for the first time.

  [^cite_note-toi0906-582]

### 10 June

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 30 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 3,696.

  [^cite_note-583]

- Lebanese media said thay Israeli strikes killed 17 people in southern Lebanon, including nine in [Tayr Debba](51745457.md), three in [Deir Qanoun an-Naher](51731210.md), two in [Seddiqin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Seddiqin), and one in [Tyre](30796.md). NNA said that a strike that hit a car in central [Sidon](28366.md) killed two others.

  [^cite_note-584]

### 11 June

- An Israeli strike hit a home in Gaza City's Moghrabi Street, killing one person. Separate Israeli strikes in the Nuseirat refugee camp killed two people.

  [^cite_note-585]

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 15 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 3,711.

  [^cite_note-586]

### 12 June

- The IDF said that it killed over 10 Hezbollah militants who served as field commanders and their successors in its strikes in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-587]

### 13 June

- The Lebanese Health Ministry raised its count of Lebanese deaths since 2 March to 3,756.

  [^cite_note-588]

### 14 June

- Six people were killed in Israeli strikes and gunfire in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-nie1406-589]

- An AP photographer said that an Israeli strike hit a five-story apartment building in the southern suburbs of Beirut, while Lebanese civil defense said it retrieved three bodies and six injured people from the scene. Israel said that it launched strikes against Hezbollah targets in response to attacks on the country.

  [^cite_note-nie1406-589]

- Israel announced that in a strike in southern Lebanon over the weekend it killed [Ali Musa Daqduq](12079041.md), a senior Hezbollah commander responsible for the [Karbala provincial headquarters raid](9129813.md) that killed five American soldiers during the [Iraq War](5043324.md).

  [^cite_note-590]

### 15 June

- Gaza health officials said that at least four people including a woman and a medic were killed in Israeli strikes in Gaza. Israel said that it killed two al-Qassam Brigades militants who were plotting attacks against its forces.

  [^cite_note-591]

- Pakistan, the primary mediator, stated that both the United States and Iran declared the "immediate and permanent termination of military operations on all fronts, including in Lebanon" as part of [the agreement](83441543.md) to end the [2026 Iran war](82533582.md). Israel stated that its forces will remain in Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-592]

  [^cite_note-593]

- Hezbollah said that it has not conducted any operations since the announcement of the agreement.

  [^cite_note-594]

### 16 June

- The Gaza Health Ministry said that the bodies of five people killed due to Israeli attacks arrived in hospitals throughout Gaza in the past 24 hours, increasing its count of Palestinian death toll in Gaza to 73,008.

  [^cite_note-595]

  [^cite_note-Gaza_death_toll_explained-2]

- At least two people were killed in an Israeli strike in the vicinity of a residential building in the Nuseirat refugee camp.

  [^cite_note-596]

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 15 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 3,798.

  [^cite_note-597]

### 17 June

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 28 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 3,826.

  [^cite_note-598]

- The IDF said that Hezbollah drone attacks in southern Lebanon injured five soldiers, one seriously.

  [^cite_note-599]

- The IDF reported killing two al-Qassam Brigades militants in Gaza, accusing them of transferring more than half a billion [NIS](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_new_shekel) for salaries for al-Qassam Brigades militants and financing attacks against Israeli civilians and soldiers and violating the ceasefire agreement.

  [^cite_note-600]

- A Hezbollah [IED](265112.md) explosion killed an IDF soldier and injured seven others near the Litani River.

  [^cite_note-601]

### 18 June

- Three people were killed in an Israeli strike on a vehicle in Gaza City's main Omar Al-Mokhtar road. The IDF said that it targeted al-Qassam Brigades militants.

  [^cite_note-602]

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 58 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 3,884.

  [^cite_note-603]

### 19 June

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 28 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 3,912.

  [^cite_note-604]

- The IDF reported that four soldiers of the [401st Brigade](2072251.md), including a Lieutenant-Colonel, were killed after Hezbollah targeted their tank with an explosive drone in the Lebanese village of [Kfar Tebnit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kfar_Tebnit), increasing the IDF death toll to 36.

  [^cite_note-605]

  [^cite_note-606]

  &#x20;The IDF said that in response it struck 80 Hezbollah sites in southern Lebanon and the Beqaa Valley, killing dozens of militants.

  [^cite_note-607]

- A Hezbollah drone strike in Kfar Tebnit injured five IDF soldiers, one seriously.

  [^cite_note-608]

- President Trump announced a renewed ceasefire between Israel and Hezbollah.

  [^cite_note-609]

### 20 June

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 83 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 4,057.

  [^cite_note-610]

- The IDF said that Hezbollah violated the ceasefire by attacking its forces in Lebanon using 50 rockets. Hezbollah said that it attacked Israeli forces who tried to seize Ali al-Taher, Nabatieh. The IDF said that in response it struck dozens of Hezbollah targets. Lebanon’s Civil Defense said that 30 people were killed including civilians and several others were injured including civilians.

  [^cite_note-611]

  [^cite_note-612]

- The IDF announced that a soldier of the [Maglan](26576560.md) Unit was killed and 13 others were injured in an overnight Hezbollah drone attack near Kfar Tebnit, increasing the IDF death toll to 37.

  [^cite_note-613]

### 21 June

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 49 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 4,106 including 251 children.

  [^cite_note-614]

### 22 June

- A 16-year-old girl was killed in an Israeli strike in a street in Gaza City's Rimal neighborhood while on her way to school. Israel said that the strike targeted an al-Qassam Brigades militant, but that it was aware of reports that an "uninvolved individual was harmed." Israeli strikes in the same area also wounded three other people.

  [^cite_note-615]

### 23 June

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 17 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 4,192.

  [^cite_note-616]

### 24 June

- An [Israeli Bedouin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Negev_Bedouin) civilian contractor for the [Defense Ministry](3226446.md) was killed in a building collapse while conducting demolition work in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-617]

- An [Israeli Druze](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Druze_in_Israel) soldier of the [7th Armoured Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/7th_Armored_Brigade_\(Israel\)) was killed and another soldier was wounded in an 'operational incident' in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-618]

### 25 June

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 19 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 4,230.

  [^cite_note-619]

- Four Israeli soldiers were wounded by a grenade during a shootout with a Hezbollah militant in [Beit Yahoun](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beit_Yahoun). The militant was killed after soldiers returned fire. The IDF added that six Hezbollah militants who "posed a threat" were killed in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-620]

### 26 June

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 13 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 4,243.

  [^cite_note-621]

- [Anti-Hamas protests](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Gaza_Strip_anti-Hamas_protests) erupted in Gaza City and other regions of northern Gaza.

  [^cite_note-622]

  &#x20;Armed Hamas operatives were deployed to quell them.

  [^cite_note-623]

- US Secretary of State [Marco Rubio](5502549.md) announced a framework deal between Israel and Lebanon that aims to achieve "lasting peace and security" through US mediation. The agreement asks for a cease-fire, with Hezbollah agreeing to terminate all hostilities and withdraw from southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-624]

  &#x20;It was later rejected by Hezbollah.

  [^cite_note-625]

### 27 June

- NNA reported an Israeli strike in the Nabatieh region.

  [^cite_note-626]

### 28 June

- The Lebanese Health Ministry increased the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 4,246.

  [^cite_note-627]

- At least four people including a 13-year-old girl were killed in Israeli strikes in Gaza. The IDF said that one of those strikes targeted an al-Qassam Brigades militant.

  [^cite_note-628]

  [^cite_note-sfgate-629]

  [^cite_note-st2906-630]

- The IDF announced that it killed several Hezbollah militants armed with [RPGs](26286.md) in the vicinity of the IDF’s buffer zone in Nabatieh district. It also said it hit a rocket launcher in the area that posed a threat to its forces.

  [^cite_note-631]

- The IDF announced that it killed a [Nukhba forces](75044554.md) militant who participated in the 7 October attacks and seized an IDF vehicle that he took to Gaza. It also said that it killed al-Qassam Brigades' [tunnel](43413500.md) unit head in Khan Yunis following a strike.

  [^cite_note-632]

- The IDF announced that one of its soldiers was killed and another soldier was slightly injured in southern Lebanon the previous day.

  [^cite_note-633]

- The IDF, the Israel Police and the [Shin Bet](311752.md) announced that they detained an [Al-Aqsa Martyrs' Brigades](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Aqsa_Martyrs'_Brigades) operative suspected of planning a [terrorist attack](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terrorist_attack) from the Balata refugee camp.

  [^cite_note-634]

- Prime Minister Benjamin Netanyahu and Defense Minister Israel Katz announced that the IDF dismantled [Hezbollah's subterranean infrastructure](78024047.md) in the Majdal Zone and informed the Trump administration in advance. They added that it was over 200 meters long and over 25 meters deep, contained hundreds of weapons and several firing shafts allegedly intended to attack Israel.

  [^cite_note-635]

### 29 June

- The Gaza Health Ministry said that the bodies of four people killed due to Israeli attacks arrived in hospitals throughout Gaza in the past 24 hours, increasing its count of Palestinian death toll in Gaza to 73,058.

  [^cite_note-636]

  [^cite_note-Gaza_death_toll_explained-2]

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 11 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 4,257.

  [^cite_note-637]

- Gaza medics said that three people including an 8-year-old child were killed and seven people were injured in an Israeli strike that hit a tent in a Deir al-Balah. The IDF said that it targeted a militant in that strike. It added that one of its strikes in southern Gaza the day before killed an al-Quds Brigades militant who took part in the 7 October attacks and abducted Israeli civilians during the attack.

  [^cite_note-sfgate-629]

  [^cite_note-st2906-630]

- The IDF announced strikes targeting militants, including three in that weekend. 12 people including four children were killed.

  [^cite_note-sfgate-629]

  [^cite_note-st2906-630]

- The IDF and [Shin Bet](311752.md) announced that they killed a senior militant within al-Qassam Brigades' apparatus in the Rafah Brigade whom it accused of advancing activities intended to harm its forces in Gaza in a strike in southern Gaza the previous week.

  [^cite_note-638]

- The IDF announced that it hit three Hezbollah command centers in Nabatieh and Mayfadoun the day before in retaliation to Hezbollah's alleged violations of the truce deal by attacking IDF forces operating in the latter’s buffer zone in Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-639]

- The IDF announced that one of its reservists was critically injured in a blast in the Beaufort Castle area.

  [^cite_note-640]

- NNA reported that Israeli fighter jets conducted bombings on the area between Qantara and Deir Seryan.

  [^cite_note-641]

- The IDF signed a \$7 million contract for AI-powered radar to thwart Hezbollah drones.

  [^cite_note-642]

### 30 June

- The Gaza Health Ministry said that eight people were killed due to Israeli attacks in Gaza in the past 24 hours, increasing its count of Palestinian death toll in Gaza to 73,066.

  [^cite_note-643]

  [^cite_note-Gaza_death_toll_explained-2]

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 21 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 4,278.

  [^cite_note-644]

- Israel announced that it improved the [Iron Dome](18342427.md) air defence system to better counter threats.

  [^cite_note-645]

- The IDF announced that it killed an al-Quds Brigades platoon commander who was involved in the 7 October attacks in a strike in Gaza the day before, adding that it took measures "to mitigate harm to civilians". A released hostage said that he was tortured under this militant's command.

  [^cite_note-646]

  [^cite_note-647]

- The IDF and [Shin Bet](311752.md) announced that they killed an al-Qassam Brigades militant who was the commander of Rafah Brigade's Yibna Battalion, and directed weapons smuggling into Gaza, adding that "steps were taken to mitigate harm to civilians."

  [^cite_note-648]

- Iran said that Iran, the US, and Lebanon have agreed to form a committee to oversee the end of the war in Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-649]

- The IDF announced that it killed a Hezbollah militant who posed a threat to its forces in Manzala, in the vicinity of Nabatieh.

  [^cite_note-650]

## July 2026

### 1 July

- The IDF and [Shin Bet](311752.md) announced that they killed an al-Qassam Brigades platoon commander whom it accused of violating the ceasefire in a southern Gaza strike.

  [^cite_note-651]

- The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 19 people were killed in Israeli attacks in the previous 24 hours, increasing the Lebanese death toll according to its count to 4,297.

  [^cite_note-652]

### 2 July

- Hamas announced that it executed a person in Gaza whom it accused of [collaborating with Israel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wartime_collaboration) and assisting in the assassination of several senior Hamas leaders, including former Hamas leader in the Gaza Strip [Izz al-Din Haddad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Izz_al-Din_Haddad).

  [^cite_note-653]

### 3 July

- The IDF announced that one of its reservists was critically injured due to a "close-quarters encounter in southern Lebanon" the day before.

  [^cite_note-654]

- The IDF announced that it killed a [Nukhba forces](75044554.md) militant who participated in the 7 October attacks and was responsible for the kidnapping of four Israeli hostages including an [Arab-Israeli](492331.md) and holding them in Hamas captivity in a strike in northern Gaza.

  [^cite_note-655]

- The IDF announced that it hit roughly 10 Hezbollah infrastructure sites and a truck in southern Lebanon in retaliation to the group's alleged ceasefire breaches.

  [^cite_note-656]

### 4 July

- The Lebanese Health Ministry increased the Lebanese death toll due to Israeli attacks according to its count to 4,303.

  [^cite_note-657]

### 5 July

- Gaza health officials said that at least two people were killed and several others were injured in an Israeli strike that hit a group of individuals at the Omar Al-Mokhtar road in central Gaza City.

  [^cite_note-658]

### 6 July

- Hamas announced the resignation of [its civil administration](38626632.md) under the [Gaza peace plan](81215079.md).

  [^cite_note-659]

- Gaza medics said that five people were killed in Israeli strikes in Gaza. The IDF said that it targeted an al-Qassam Brigades militant and an al-Quds Brigades militant in those strikes.

  [^cite_note-660]

- The Lebanese Health Ministry increased the Lebanese death toll due to Israeli attacks since 2 March according to its count to 4,319.

  [^cite_note-661]

- Lebanese Health Ministry reported that an Israeli strike hit a vehicle in Nabatieh al Fawqa, killing four people including at least two women.

  [^cite_note-662]

- The IDF announced that it targeted four suspects who approached its security zone in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-663]

### 8 July

- The Gaza Health Ministry increased its count of Palestinian death toll due to Israeli attacks in Gaza to 73,110.

  [^cite_note-664]

  [^cite_note-Gaza_death_toll_explained-2]

- Gaza health officials said that nine people including two children were killed in Israeli strikes in Gaza. The IDF said that one of those strikes was aimed at militants.

  [^cite_note-665]

- The IDF announced that it killed a Hezbollah militant at a building in the vicinity of Bint Jbeil in the IDF buffer zone in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-666]

- The IDF announced that it killed in a southern Gaza strike a [Nukhba Force](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nukhba_Force) militant who was involved in the 7 October attacks and accused him of violating the ceasefire, adding that it took measures "to mitigate harm to civilians."

  [^cite_note-667]

### 9 July

- Gaza civil defense said that six people were killed in Israeli strikes in Gaza. One of those strikes targeted a Hamas spokesperson, but killed his bodyguard instead.

  [^cite_note-668]

- The Lebanese Health Ministry increased the Lebanese death toll due to Israeli attacks since 2 March according to its count to 4,321.

  [^cite_note-669]

- The IDF announced that it and [Shin Bet](311752.md) killed in a strike in Khan Yunis an al-Qassam Brigades militant who was involved in the 7 October attacks, abducted Israelis and helped keep them hostage in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-670]

- The IDF announced that it killed an al-Quds Brigades production head "responsible for efforts to manufacture weapons that were intended for use against IDF troops and Israeli civilians" in a southern Gaza strike. It added that it killed an al-Qassam Brigades sniper in a northern Gaza strike.

  [^cite_note-671]

- The IDF announced that it destroyed two [tunnels](78024047.md) with a combined length of roughly 200 m (660 ft) in Majdal Zoun and seized "dozens" of armaments.

  [^cite_note-672]

### 10 July

- Witnesses and the local truckers' organization said that an IDF soldier in Rafah killed a Palestinian truck driver bringing food aid from the [World Central Kitchen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_Central_Kitchen) into Gaza. The IDF confirmed the shooting saying that it opened fire "after perceiving an immediate threat".

  [^cite_note-673]

- The IDF announced that it killed in a southern Gaza strike, a commander of a [Nukhba forces](75044554.md) cell that infiltrated the Re'im Army Base in southern Israel during the 7 October attacks and accused him of violating the ceasefire.

  [^cite_note-674]

### 12 July

- The Gaza Health Ministry increased its count of Palestinian death toll due to Israeli attacks in Gaza to 73,221.

  [^cite_note-ddn1207-675]

  [^cite_note-Gaza_death_toll_explained-2]

- A 9-year-old girl was killed when gunfire was directed at a tent encampment on Bureij refugee camp's eastern side. The IDF said that it was not aware of the gunfire. Four people were killed and 14 others were injured in Israeli strikes on a metal foundry in Sabra neighborhood of Gaza City. The IDF said that it had struck al-Qassam Brigades militants operating inside an armaments manufacturing site twice, in breach of the truce by the Brigades. At least one person was killed and several others were injured including children in an Israeli strike that hit a tent encampment in al-Mawasi. The IDF said that it conducted a strike in that area, but was unaware of casualties.

  [^cite_note-676]

  [^cite_note-ddn1207-675]

- The IDF declared that it killed two al-Qassam Brigades militants in two separate Gaza strikes.

  [^cite_note-677]

### 13 July

- The IDF published a visual of what it described as hidden [Hezbollah tunnels](78024047.md) beneath Beaufort Castle, where it said the group's command centres were situated.

  [^cite_note-678]

- The UN accused the al-Qassam Brigades militants of obstructing Gaza humanitarian deliveries, which the latter denied.

  [^cite_note-679]

  [^cite_note-680]

### 14 July

- Medics and police officials and the [Hamas-led interior ministry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Interior_\(Palestine\)) stated that a 10-year-old boy was killed in Rafah, and at least seven people, including woman and a senior [Hamas policeman](81695807.md) were killed and several other Hamas police officers were injured in an Israeli strike that hit a Hamas-led police force post in Jabalia. Gaza medics said that one person was killed and three others were injured in an Israeli strike in Khan Yunis. The IDF stated that it targeted an al-Qassam Brigades militant.

  [^cite_note-681]

  [^cite_note-682]

- The IDF announced that it killed a commander of an al-Qassam Brigades' Naval Array cell and killed three other militants who posed an immediate threat to its forces in a Gaza City strike.

  [^cite_note-683]

- The IDF announced that it killed al-Qassam Brigades' central Jabaliya battalion military security chief in a northern Gaza strike. It also stated that it killed a [Nukhba](75044554.md) militant in Hamas' central Jabaliya battalion who also served as Hamas police investigations department head, another militant in the same battalion and a militant in the western Jabaliya battalion who also worked in the Hamas police force.

  [^cite_note-684]

### 15 July

- Gaza health officials said that three people including a woman and a 6-year-old girl were killed and one person was wounded in an Israeli strike that hit an apartment building in Deir Al-Balah. The IDF said that it aimed at an al-Qassam Brigades militant. Gaza medics said that one person was killed in another strike in Gaza City's Sheikh Radwan neighbourhood.

  [^cite_note-685]

- The IDF announced that it killed a [Nukhba forces](75044554.md) company deputy commander and the chief of Nukhba cell in al-Qassam Brigades' Sabra Battalion in a northern Gaza strike.

  [^cite_note-686]

- The IDF announced that it killed in a strike a militant from al-Qassam Brigades' Eastern Khan Yunis Battalion who was involved in the 7 October attacks and took three IDF soldiers as hostages.

  [^cite_note-687]

### 16 July

- Gaza health ‌officials said that five people were killed in Israeli strikes in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-688]

- Syria stated that it thwarted an attempt to transfer armaments to Hezbollah through Iraq.

  [^cite_note-689]

- The IDF said it hit four al-Qassam Brigades arms depots in Gaza overnight.

  [^cite_note-690]

### 17 July

- Gaza health officials said that at least eight people were killed and more than 20 others injured in an Israeli strike in Nuseirat refugee camp when they attended a funeral of a person killed in prior Israeli strike strike that killed two people and the IDF said that it targeted an al-Qassam Brigades militant in that strike. The IDF stated that it aimed at an al-Quds Brigades militant cell and aware of claims for harm to civilians. Three more people including two women were killed in apparent Israeli strikes in northern Gaza, Gaza City and Khan Yunis. The IDF issued evacuation order for an area east of Deir al-Balah.

  [^cite_note-691]

  [^cite_note-692]

- The IDF stated that it killed an al-Qassam Brigades commander who took part in holding Israeli hostages and also served as its Khan Younis Brigade propaganda activities leader and as the Brigade leader [Mohammed Deif](92534.md)'s senior aide.

  [^cite_note-693]

### 18 July

- At least five people including three children aged between 8 and 18 were killed and six others were injured including four children of 8 to 16 years of age in an Israeli strike that hit an apartment in Nasr neighborhood of Gaza City. The IDF said it targeted al-Qassam Brigades infrastructure and had spotted al-Qassam Brigades militants in the location. Four people were killed and another person was seriously injured in an Israeli strike on a group of individuals in Gaza City's Zeitoun neighborhood. The IDF said it targeted an al-Qassam Brigades militant.

  [^cite_note-694]

- The IDF stated that it hit a Hezbollah cell in the vicinity of Tebnit after it identified a Hezbollah [UAV](58900.md), accusing Hezbollah of breaching truce understandings.

  [^cite_note-695]

- The IDF stated that an al-Quds Brigades sniper and a commander in al-Qassam Brigades' operations headquarters accused of violating the ceasefire were killed in strikes in central and north Gaza.

  [^cite_note-696]

### 20 July

- The Lebanese Health Ministry increased the Lebanese death toll due to Israeli attacks since 2 March according to its count to 4,328.

  [^cite_note-697]

- Hamas announced that it elected [Khalil al-Hayya](43349601.md) as [its overall leader](77528143.md).

  [^cite_note-698]

- NNA reported a large-scale Israeli attack in Kfar Tebnit.

  [^cite_note-699]

- The US announced that Israel handed over sections of its buffer zone in southern Lebanon to the [Lebanese government](78886120.md) as part of the [2026 Israel–Lebanon ceasefire](82970933.md).

  [^cite_note-700]

### 21 July

- Six members of a family including a woman, three girls and one boy were killed in Israeli strike that hit a residential building in Sabra neighbourhood of Gaza City. The IDF stated that it targeted an al-Qassam Brigades militant.

  [^cite_note-reuters2107-701]

- At least six people were killed and others were injured in Israeli strikes on a vehicle in central Gaza and an association in Gaza City.

  [^cite_note-reuters2107-701]

- Lebanon announced that the Lebanese Army started deploying in [Zawtar al-Gharbiyah](78327596.md) following Israeli withdrawal from the town in accordance with the [2026 Israel–Lebanon ceasefire](82970933.md).

- The IDF announced that it and [Shin Bet](311752.md) killed an al-Qassam Brigades militant who served as its Gaza City Brigade operations head during the 7 October attacks and held numerous hostages including women following a Gaza City strike.

- The IDF announced that it killed an al-Qassam Brigades cell commander who took hostages during the 7 October attacks.

  [^cite_note-704]

- The Gaza Health Ministry increased its count of the Palestinian death toll due to Israeli attacks in Gaza to 73,305.

- Two people were killed in an Israeli strike in northern Gaza. The IDF stated that it targeted militants.

- The Lebanese Health Ministry increased the Lebanese death toll due to Israeli attacks since 2 March according to its count to 4,330.

  [^cite_note-709]

### 25 July

- The Gaza Health Ministry increased its count of the Palestinian death toll due to Israeli attacks in Gaza to 73,317.

  [^cite_note-710]

  [^cite_note-Gaza_death_toll_explained-2]

- An Israeli strike in Khan Yunis killed a [high school](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Education_in_Palestine) student. [Hamas-led police force](81695807.md) chief, Abdel-Nasser Al-Maqadma, was killed in an Israeli strike in Gaza City's Sheikh Radwan neighborhood. The IDF stated that he was also a militant from al-Qassam Brigades. Another apparent Israeli strike in Nuseirat refugee camp killed a medic. Two people were killed in an Israeli strike that hit a house in Khan Yunis. The IDF announced that it targeted an al-Quds Brigades militant. Another police officer injured in an Israeli strike the previous week died.

  [^cite_note-711]

### 26 July

- The Gaza Health Ministry increased its count of the Palestinian death toll due to Israeli attacks in Gaza to 73,326.

  [^cite_note-712]

  [^cite_note-Gaza_death_toll_explained-2]

  [^cite_note-713]
