  [^cite_note-714]

  [^cite_note-715]

### 27 July

- 60 Palestinian detainees were released by Israel into Gaza.

  [^cite_note-716]

- The IDF and [Shin Bet](311752.md) stated that they killed an al-Qassam Brigades militant who served as the Brigade's internal security chief in a strike in central Gaza and accused him of violating the ceasefire.

  [^cite_note-717]

- The IDF announced that it killed an al-Quds Brigades Nukhba militant whom it accused of breaching the truce and another al-Qassam Brigades commander in its separate Gaza strikes.

  [^cite_note-718]

  &#x20;It added that it dismantled an al-Qassam Brigades northern Gaza [tunnel network](43413500.md) situated east of the Yellow Line in an area under its control and it demolished additional tunnel route measuring several hundred meters.

  [^cite_note-719]

### 29 July

- The IDF said that a Hezbollah drone attacked one of its engineering vehicles in southern Lebanon. Lebanese media, citing a local person, reported that the group hit the vehicle to prevent Israel from carrying out groundworks in the south of the country.

  [^cite_note-720]

### 30 July

- At least four people including an 18-month-old child and an eight-year-old girl were killed in Israeli strikes in Gaza. The IDF stated that it targeted al-Qassam Brigades militants.

  [^cite_note-721]

- The IDF stated that it demolished a [Hezbollah tunnel network](78024047.md) sponsored by the [Government of Iran](50913538.md) in the Beaufort area.

  [^cite_note-722]

### 31 July

- President Trump said that his [Board of Peace](81274522.md) had reached an agreement to disarm Hamas and [other militant organisations in Gaza](75192468.md). Hamas leader [Ghazi Hamad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ghazi_Hamad) declared that "draft agreement" announced by Trump calls for the transfer of heavy weaponry in Gaza to a Palestinian administration following Israeli withdrawal from Gaza.

  [^cite_note-723]

  [^cite_note-724]

  [^cite_note-725]

- Israeli strikes and gunfire in Gaza killed one person and injured several others.

  [^cite_note-reuters3107-726]

- The IDF stated that a strike intended to target an al-Qassam Brigades militant was diverted after other people were spotted.

  [^cite_note-reuters3107-726]

- The IDF and [Shin Bet](311752.md) stated that they killed in a Gaza City strike an al-Qassam Brigades deputy cell commander who was involved in holding hostage an Israeli woman and the remains of a female IDF soldier and accused him of using civil facilities for military purposes and targeting Israeli civilians during the war.

  [^cite_note-727]

## August 2026

### 1 August

- Two people were killed in Israeli strikes in Gaza. The IDF stated that it targeted al-Qassam Brigades militants. The Gaza Health Ministry stated that a separate Israeli strike destroyed medicine warehouses used by Al-Aqsa Martyrs Hospital.

  [^cite_note-728]

- The IDF announced that one of its officers was moderately injured amidst an operational activity in Lebanon's south.

  [^cite_note-729]

### 2 August

- At least 17 people including children were killed in Israeli strikes in Gaza. The IDF announced that its targets were militants.

  [^cite_note-730]

- The IDF stated that it killed two al-Qassam Brigades commanders, including a [Nukhba forces](75044554.md) commander involved in the 7 October attacks in its strikes in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-731]

- The al-Quds Brigades agreed to the [Board of Peace](81274522.md) disarmament plan for Gaza.

  [^cite_note-732]

- The IDF said that it killed several [Army of Islam](5883418.md) militants in a Gaza strike.

  [^cite_note-733]

- The 401st Brigade of the IDF claimed to have destroyed 1,200 Hezbollah tunnels, concluding their eight-month deployment.

  [^cite_note-734]

### 3 August

- The Gaza Health Ministry increased its count of the Palestinian death toll due to Israeli attacks in Gaza to 73,375.

  [^cite_note-735]

  [^cite_note-Gaza_death_toll_explained-2]

- The IDF announced that it killed an al-Quds Brigades commander who invaded Israel amidst the 7 October attacks and held an Israeli hostage in a Deir el-Balah strike.

  [^cite_note-736]

### 4 August

- The remains of 112 people including children and women from the same clan were found from the debris of a structure in Gaza City's Sabra neighborhood which was hit in an Israeli strike during the war.

  [^cite_note-737]

  [^cite_note-738]

- The Israeli government ordered the IDF not to launch strikes in Gaza without the authorization of IDF chief of staff Lt. Gen. [Eyal Zamir](61877756.md), following US pressure to limit Israel's operations in Gaza, allowing forces to strike without such approval only in "imminent danger".

  [^cite_note-739]

  [^cite_note-740]

### 5 August

- The IDF said that two of its reservists were killed, and four were critically injured after being hit by an IED in Majdal Zoun. The IDF ordered to evacuate [al-Mansouri](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Mansouri), citing alleged "Hezbollah violations of truce". It later stated that it executed precision strikes in Lebanon's south.

  [^cite_note-741]

  [^cite_note-742]

  [^cite_note-743]

  &#x20;It later announced that it struck Hezbollah infrastructure.

  [^cite_note-744]

### 7 August

- Lebanon and Israel agreed on a shortlist of states that could send forces to verify Hezbollah disarmament.

  [^cite_note-745]

- A Hezbollah drone struck in the vicinity of Israeli soldiers in the Ali Taher Ridge in Lebanon's south in an apparent Hezbollah breach of the truce.

  [^cite_note-746]

### 8 August

- The Gaza Health Ministry increased its count of the Palestinian death toll due to Israeli attacks in Gaza to 73,384.

  [^cite_note-747]

  [^cite_note-Gaza_death_toll_explained-2]

- The IDF said that one of its soldiers was moderately injured in an "operational accident" in Lebanon's south.

  [^cite_note-748]

- Lebanon stated that Israeli soldiers crossed into [Zawtar al-Gharbiyah](78327596.md) and constructed an earthen barrier. The IDF stated that it was unaware of such an incident.

  [^cite_note-749]

### 9 August

- The Gaza Health Ministry stated that one person was killed in Israeli attacks in the last 24 hours while the remains of another individual killed in a prior Israeli attack was recovered from rubble, increasing its count of the Palestinian death toll due to Israeli attacks in Gaza to 73,385.

  [^cite_note-750]

  [^cite_note-Gaza_death_toll_explained-2]

- The Lebanese Health Ministry increased the Lebanese death toll due to Israeli attacks since 2 March according to its count to 4,335.

  [^cite_note-751]

- The IDF and [Shin Bet](311752.md) admitted for the first time a special mission to target and kill all 7 October militants, adding that over 2,700 out of an estimated 5,000 militants who took part in the attacks have been confirmed by the IDF to have been killed.

  [^cite_note-752]

- Prime Minister Netanyahu announced Israel’s rejection of the deal drafted by President Trump's Gaza [Board of Peace](81274522.md).

  [^cite_note-753]

### 11 August

- NNA reported that an Israeli drone attack targeted a vehicle on Nabatieh's outskirts, adding that an ambulance team from the [Hezbollah-connected](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hezbollah_social_services) Islamic Health Authority, Islamic Message Scouts Association, and other medical staff was bombed in another attack while transporting a wounded individual in the area, injuring two people and damaging an ambulance. The IDF denied responsibility.

  [^cite_note-754]

  [^cite_note-755]

### 12 August

- The IDF stated that it mistakenly fired an interceptor at its soldiers in Lebanon's south, without causing casualties.

  [^cite_note-756]

- One person was seriously injured in an Israeli strike while riding on a rickshaw in Beit Lahia. The IDF stated that it targeted an al-Qassam Brigades commander whom it accused of violating the ceasefire.

  [^cite_note-757]

### 13 August

- The mandate of the [European Union Border Assistance Mission to Rafah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_Union_Border_Assistance_Mission_to_Rafah) and the [European Union Police Mission for the Palestinian Territories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_Union_Police_Mission_for_the_Palestinian_Territories) at the [Rafah crossing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rafah_crossing) was extended by Israel till the end of June next year.

  [^cite_note-758]

- Two people including a senior [police](81695807.md) official were killed in Israeli strikes in Gaza. The IDF stated that one of those strikes targeted an al-Qasam Brigades commander whom it accused of violating the truce.

  [^cite_note-759]

### 14 August

- The IDF announced that it killed an al-Qassam Brigades company commander in a Khan Yunis strike the day before.

  [^cite_note-760]

### 15 August

- The IDF stated that an explosive device struck one of its tanks in southern Gaza. It added that it killed two militants.

  [^cite_note-761]

- Lebanese President [Joseph Aoun](53467128.md) and Lebanese media announced that an Israeli attack in Ansar killed seven people and injured three others, all members of same family including children and women.

  [^cite_note-762]

  [^cite_note-763]

  [^cite_note-764]

  &#x20;It also reported that a wave of attacks occurred in the Nabatieh area, including on the Ali Taher Ridge, under which Hezbollah has a large [tunnel](78024047.md).

  [^cite_note-767]

- The IDF announced that it killed a Hezbollah’s Badr regional division's senior commander in a strike in [Deir al-Zahrani](68872502.md) the previous day.

- The Gaza Health Ministry stated that the remains of five people killed in Israeli attacks arrived at hospitals throughout Gaza, increasing its count of the Palestinian death toll due to Israeli attacks in Gaza to 73,399.

  [^cite_note-Gaza_death_toll_explained-2]

  [^cite_note-777]

- The IDF stated that it killed an al-Quds Brigade militant who entered Israel as part of the 7 October attacks and took an Israeli as hostage.

- At least nine people including a 13-year-old girl were killed and 15 others were injured in an Israeli strike on municipal [police](81695807.md) headquarters in Gaza. One person was killed in a separate Israeli strike in Nuseirat refugee camp. The IDF said that it struck an al-Qassam Brigades militant.

- The US imposed sanctions against Hezbollah, citing its actions on behalf of the [Government of Iran](50913538.md).

### 21 August

- The IDF stated that it hit a structure in [Baraashit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Baraashit) after suspects "posing threat" entered there.

### 23 August

- The Gaza Health Ministry increased its count of the Palestinian death toll due to Israeli attacks in Gaza to 73,420.

  [^cite_note-783]

  [^cite_note-Gaza_death_toll_explained-2]

### 24 August

- A 10-year-old girl was killed in apparent Israeli fire in Khan Yunis. The IDF stated that it was unaware of the event. Separate Israeli strikes on a home in Bureij refugee camp and a ⁠street in Khan Yunis killed two people including a four-year-old boy and injured his mother and sister. One individual was killed and another was injured in an Israeli strike that hit a tent in Deir Al-Balah. The IDF stated that it targeted a [Nukhba forces](75044554.md) militant. The IDF also stated that it conducted strikes in the Shati refugee camp and in Deir al-Balah following warnings, targeting what it cast as two al-Qassam Brigades arms depots inside mosques. It did not provide proof but said the facilities stored dozens of “Kalashnikov rifles, magazines and additional military equipment.”

  [^cite_note-786]

  [^cite_note-787]

- The head of the [Sha'ar HaNegev Regional Council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sha'ar_HaNegev_Regional_Council) announced that a kite presumably launched from Gaza landed in Nahal Oz.

  [^cite_note-788]

- The popular committees, which are made up of local NGO's and charity representatives that run Gaza displacement camps and shelters warned parents to cease their children flying kites in a declaration coordinated with the [Hamas government](38626632.md).

  [^cite_note-789]

### 26 August

- The IDF stated that it hit an al-Qassam Brigades site in the vicinity of Kamal Adwan Hospital and a school in Jabalia.

  [^cite_note-790]

### 27 August

- The IDF accused Hezbollah of firing two explosive UAVs towards its soldiers in Ali Taher Ridge, adding that it hit Hezbollah munitions storage facilities after the attack.

  [^cite_note-791]

  [^cite_note-792]

  &#x20;NNA reported that Israel attacks in [Arabsalim](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabsalim) killed a woman and injured others.

  [^cite_note-793]

- Prime Minister Netanyahu called off the IDF opening of the [Erez border crossing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Erez_border_crossing).

  [^cite_note-794]

- The IDF stated that it killed two [Nukhba forces](75044554.md) militants who infiltrated its Re'im camp in southern Israel amidst the 7 October attacks.

  [^cite_note-795]

### 28 August

- Local health officials said that five people were killed in Israeli strikes in Gaza. The IDF confirmed that it hit two separate parts of the Gaza City, adding that it killed an al-Qassam Brigades militant and his two accomplices in a strike on a car in Jenin. al-Qassam Brigades and al-Quds Brigades confirmed one of them as their member.

  [^cite_note-796]

  [^cite_note-797]

- The Lebanese Health Ministry increased the Lebanese death toll due to Israeli attacks since 2 March according to its count to 4,352.

  [^cite_note-798]

- The IDF announced it killed an al-Qassam Brigades anti-tank unit commander and a member of the Khan Yunis brigade following strikes in Shati and Khan Yunis respectively and accused them of violating the ceasefire.

  [^cite_note-799]

- The IDF declared that it killed an al-Qassam Brigades commander who took part in the 7 October attacks and a PIJ observation platoon commander in different Gaza strikes and accused them of violating the truce.

  [^cite_note-800]

### 29 August

- Lebanese media reported that Israel conducted an attack in Nabatieh al-Fawqa.

  [^cite_note-801]

- The IDF stated that it targeted an al-Qassam Brigades commander in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-802]

### 30 August

- Two people including a three-year-old boy were killed and several others were injured in an Israeli strike in the vicinity of a bakery in Deir Al-Balah. The IDF stated that one of those killed was an al-Qassam Brigades militant and accused him of violating the truce.

  [^cite_note-803]

- The IDF announced that it targeted two [Nukhba forces](75044554.md) militants whom it accused of breaching the truce in a Khan Yunis strike.

  [^cite_note-804]

- The IDF declared that it killed two al-Qassam Brigades militants including a Nukhba forces commander who was involved in the 7 October attacks in a northern Gaza strike.

  [^cite_note-805]

- Lebanese media reported that Israeli strikes hit Yohmor.

  [^cite_note-806]

## September 2026

### 1 September

- The IDF announced that [Shin Bet](311752.md) special forces arrested the head of al-Qassam Brigades' internal security service from Gaza City in a joint operation. Hamas-run [ Gaza Interior Ministry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Interior_\(Palestine\)) confirmed that he was wounded and captured by Israel. Netanyahu declared that he was responsible for concealing and transporting Israeli hostages captured during the 7 October attacks. At least four people ‌including ⁠two children were killed and several others were injured in Israeli strikes during the operation. A child was killed in apparent Israeli fire in central Gaza. Another person was killed in a Jabalia strike.

  [^cite_note-807]

### 2 September

- The IDF announced that it killed an al-Qassam Brigades' Military Security Department in its Gaza City Brigade and another al-Qassam Brigades militant in a strike in Gaza and accused them of breaching the truce.

  [^cite_note-808]

- The IDF stated that Hezbollah fired two drones towards its soldiers in south Lebanon's Ali Taher Ridge overnight, adding that the group’s facilities were hit in retaliation.

  [^cite_note-809]

### 3 September

- The IDF declared that it killed a militant who posed 'immediate threat' in Gaza's north.

  [^cite_note-810]

- The IDF stated that it killed two people who entered the Yellow Line.

  [^cite_note-811]

- Prime Minister Netanyahu announced that five Lebanese civilians were released by Israel in exchange for information on Jewish remains in Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-812]

### 4 September

- The IDF stated that it carried out strikes in northern Gaza after its soldiers were attacked in the vicinity of the Yellow Line, calling it a breach of the truce by militants.

  [^cite_note-813]

- The IDF and Shin Bet stated they killed an al-Qassam Brigades commander who attacked the Sufa base in southern Israel during the 7 October attacks.

  [^cite_note-814]

- NNA reported that two people traveling in a bike were killed in an Israeli strike in Nabatieh.

  [^cite_note-815]

- The IDF stated that it launched strikes on Hezbollah targets after an explosive UAV was fired at its forces in Lebanon's south. The Lebanese Health Ministry stated that those attacks killed three people and wounded 23 others including medics, children and women.

  [^cite_note-816]

  [^cite_note-817]

### 5 September

- The IDF announced that it mistakenly fired an interceptor missile in response to its own gunfire in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-818]

### 6 September

- Two people including an eight-year-girl were killed and six others were injured in an Israeli strike on a vehicle in western Gaza City. The IDF stated that it targeted an al-Qassam Brigades militant.

  [^cite_note-819]

- The IDF stated that it launched strikes on targets in Lebanon's south after Hezbollah fired two explosive UAVs toward its soldiers deployed in the Ali Taher Ridge, describing it as a truce breach by Hezbollah militants. It later issued an evacuation order for [Arabsalim](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabsalim), saying that a structure in the town is owned by Hezbollah. Lebanon's Health Ministry announced that four people were killed and 20 others were injured in those attacks, adding that a hospital was destroyed in an Israeli strike in the vicinity of the Ridge.

  [^cite_note-820]

  [^cite_note-821]

  [^cite_note-822]

- Lebanese media reported that an Israeli strike in Nabatieh killed three people.

  [^cite_note-823]

### 7 September

- The IDF ordered to evacuate a specific area in Deir al-Zahrani, saying that its target is a facility used by Hezbollah militants. Israeli strikes were later reported in the region.

  [^cite_note-824]

  &#x20;NNA reported that separate Israeli strikes in Lebanon's south killed at least 11 people including two children and two paramedics with the [Amal Movement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amal_Movement)-affiliated Risala Scouts and injured five others.

  [^cite_note-825]

- The IDF stated that Hezbollah fired explosive UAVs at its soldiers in Ali Taher Ridge area for the third time in seven days, triggering strikes targeting militants in retaliation.

  [^cite_note-826]

- The IDF stated that two of its soldiers were slightly injured in a clash with a Hezbollah militant who was killed in the vicinity of Beaufort Castle.

  [^cite_note-827]

### 10 September

- Four people including an eight year-old girl, a twelve year-old girl and a woman were killed in an Israeli strike that hit a house in Beit Lahia. The IDF stated that one of those killed was a militant who was involved in direct combat against its soldiers.

  [^cite_note-828]

- Prime Minister Netanyahu announced that the IDF destroyed a strategic [subterranean Hezbollah complex](78024047.md) at the Ali Taher Ridge.

  [^cite_note-829]

### 11 September

- The al-Qassam Brigades announced that one of its members who served in the brigade higher leadership council and as its Khan Yunis Brigade commander was killed in an Israeli strike, adding that he played a significant role in planning the 7 October attacks. The IDF declared that he was killed in a Khan Yunis strike, adding that a company commander and another al-Qassam Brigades militant were also killed in that strike.

  [^cite_note-830]

  [^cite_note-831]

- The IDF and [Shin Bet](311752.md) announced that they killed the leader of a sniper cell in al-Qassam Brigades' Rafah Brigade in a Khan Yunis strike and accused him of breaching the truce.

  [^cite_note-832]

### 13 September

- At least two people were killed and 13 others were injured in an Israeli strike on a vehicle in Gaza City's Tel Al-Hawa neighbourhood. The IDF announced that it targeted two al-Qassam Brigades militants.

  [^cite_note-833]

- NNA reported that Israel conducted strikes in the vicinity of Nabatieh al-Fawqa and Tel al-Dabsha.

  [^cite_note-834]

### 15 September

- The IDF declared that it killed two al-Qassam Brigades commanders in two Gaza strikes and one of them was involved in training Brigade militants before the 7 October attacks. It also stated that it killed an al-Qassam Brigades militant who took part in producing munitions for the group.

  [^cite_note-835]

- The IDF and [Shin Bet](311752.md) stated that they killed an al-Qassam Brigades militant who served as commander of Rafah Brigade, chief of the Brigade operations array and participated in the 7 October attacks in a southern Gaza strike.

  [^cite_note-836]

### 16 September

- A five-story apartment building collapsed from damage caused in prior Israeli strikes in [Gaza City](55387.md), killing at least 21 people, including 11 children and five women, and wounded at least 14 others.

  [^cite_note-837]

- The IDF announced that it killed an al-Qassam Brigades militant in a Gaza City bombing and accused him of breaching the truce.

  [^cite_note-838]

### 17 September

- The IDF declared that it killed two [Nukhba forces](75044554.md) commanders in Gaza's north.

  [^cite_note-839]

- The IDF stated it killed a Nukhba commander who participated in the 7 October attacks.

  [^cite_note-840]

- The Lebanese Health Ministry increased the Lebanese death toll due to Israeli attacks since 2 March according to its count to 4,386.

  [^cite_note-841]

### 19 September

- The IDF declared that it killed an al-Qassam Brigades commander who was involved in the 7 October attacks in a Khan Yunis strike.

  [^cite_note-842]

- The IDF announced that it hit Hezbollah targets after the latter's explosive device slightly injured two of its soldiers in its buffer zone in Lebanon's south.

  [^cite_note-843]

### 20 September

- The IDF and Shin Bet announced that they killed an al-Qassam Brigades militant who took part in the 7 October attacks, as well as two other militants from the Brigade.

  [^cite_note-844]

  [^cite_note-845]

### 21 September

- The IDF stated that it struck al-Qassam Brigades infrastructure in Gaza after one of its engineering vehicles drove over an explosive device amidst a routine operation in the Yellow Line, without causing casualties.

  [^cite_note-846]

### 22 September

- The Gaza Health Ministry increased its count of the Palestinian death toll due to Israeli attacks in Gaza to 73,919.

  [^cite_note-847]

  [^cite_note-Gaza_death_toll_explained-2]

### 23 September

- At least four people were killed and two were wounded in Israeli strikes in Gaza. Israeli officials stated that one of the killed was Hamas finance chief Mohammad Abu Elwan.

  [^cite_note-848]

### 25 September

- The IDF stated that two of its soldiers were killed by an explosion of its own drone in a Gaza outpost amidst an operation.

  [^cite_note-849]

- The IDF stated that it fired an interceptor against a “suspicious aerial target” spotted above a south Lebanon area where its soldiers are deployed.

  [^cite_note-850]

### 26 September

- The IDF stated that it bombed a Hezbollah munitions cache to "remove a threat" to its soldiers deployed in its security zone in Lebanon's south.

  [^cite_note-851]

### 27 September

- The IDF stated that Hezbollah targeted its soldiers operating in its buffer zone in Lebanon's south with an explosive drone, triggering strikes on the latter's sites in retaliation.

  [^cite_note-852]

### 28 September

- An Israeli strike hit the rooftop of a building in Gaza City, killing two people including a woman and injuring others. A woman was killed and others were injured in another Israeli strike in Nuseirat refugee camp. The IDF stated that it targeted a militant in Nuseirat.

  [^cite_note-853]

- The IDF announced completion of a major operation to dismantle Hezbollah facilities in Al-Mansouri, a town situated inside its security zone in Lebanon's south.

  [^cite_note-854]

- The IDF stated that it fired an interceptor against a "suspicious aerial target" above its soldiers in Lebanese territory.

  [^cite_note-855]

### 29 September

- The IDF and Hamas confirmed the death of Izz al-Din Al-Beik, al-Qassam Brigades chief in Gaza's north in an Israel strike on an apartment building in Nasser neighbourhood ⁠of Gaza City, whom Prime Minister Netanyahu and Defence Minister Katz accused of violating the ceasefire. Gaza health officials stated that another individual died from their injuries following the strike. Hamas announced that he co-planned the 7 October attacks. At least three people were killed by two separate Israeli strikes in Jabalia refugee camp. The IDF declared that those strikes targeted militants.

  [^cite_note-856]

### 30 September

- At least seven people including a woman were killed and others were injured in Israeli strikes in Gaza. The IDF announced that one of those strikes targeted an al-Qassam Brigades militant who intended to violate the ceasefire.

  [^cite_note-857]

  [^cite_note-858]

## October 2026

### 1 October

- The IDF launched a dedicated website and stated that a probe into 170 people killed in Gaza who had been publicly identified as journalists revealed they were active militants for militant groups. According to the IDF, 118 were members of al-Qassam Brigades, 49 belonged to al-Quds Brigades, two were with the Al-Nasser Salah al-Deen Brigades, and one was with the Mujahideen Brigades. The IDF stated that 17 of the individuals had participated in the 7 October attacks.

  [^cite_note-859]

  [^cite_note-860]

### 2 October

- The US imposed sanctions on Hamas' financing network, which includes two French-based funds.

  [^cite_note-861]

### 3 October

- The IDF declared that it has killed more than 24,000 militants in Gaza.

  [^cite_note-862]

- At least five people including a girl and three women were killed in an Israeli strike that hit an apartment in Gaza City. De facto leader of Hamas in the Gaza Strip [Ali al-Amoudi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ali_al-Amoudi) was targeted in the strike. Those killed include two members of Gaza's Greek Orthodox church.

  [^cite_note-863]

  [^cite_note-864]

- The IDF announced that eight of its soldiers were slightly injured after two vehicles crashed into each other in Rab Thalathin.

  [^cite_note-865]

### 4 October

-

A senior figure in the armed wing of Hamas, a Hamas policeman, and a senior figure in the militant group Islamic Jihad were among at least ten people killed on Thursday in a series of Israeli strikes across Gaza, according to local sources. Local medics and residents reported the deaths of senior Islamic Jihad operative Ashraf Al-Khatib in Nuseirat and a Hamas policeman in Gaza city. A Hamas source said that local commander Mohammed Al-Holy was killed in Deir al-Balah, earlier in the day. Health officials said the deaths included a 16-year-old.
